{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CekaK6yaGmFyWzUoDduDb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EeYY9hZMUZHbUMJUsU7h6o","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_cuda_r3/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                                      \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                            \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block covers BX cells in x (one full warp per row) and BY rows in y.\n// Each thread sweeps a column of ZT cells in z, keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Grid spacing enters the Laplacian as a division by d*d. Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr long long idx3(const long long x, const long long y, const long long z,\n                                                    const long long nx, const long long ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Sweeps a z-column of the grid, computing the 7-point Laplacian of f with clamped\n// boundary conditions and handing (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op.\ntemplate <typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const long long z0 = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const long long z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const long long x = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const long long y = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const long long xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const long long yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const long long slice = nx * ny;\n    const long long base = yc * nx + xc;\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n\n    double cm, c0, cp;\n    c0 = f[base + z0 * slice];\n    cm = (z0 > 0) ? f[base + (z0 - 1) * slice] : c0;\n    cp = (z0 + 1 < nz) ? f[base + (z0 + 1) * slice] : c0;\n\n    for (long long z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const long long idx = base + z * slice;\n\n        // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n        double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n        if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n        else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n        const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n        const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n        const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n        const double lap = scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n                           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n                           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n\n        if (active) op(idx, c0, lap);\n\n        if (z + 1 < z1) {  // roll the z-window forward\n            cm = c0;\n            c0 = cp;\n            cp = (z + 2 < nz) ? f[base + (z + 2) * slice] : c0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const long long idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double D, const double dt) {\n    laplacianSweep(mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const long long idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t linear_id = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x;\n    if (linear_id >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// A division by k is bit-identical to a multiplication by 1/k exactly when k is a\n// power of two (the reciprocal is then exact).\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double k) {\n    int exp = 0;\n    return k > 0.0 && std::isfinite(k) && std::frexp(k, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int                                               const long long nx, const long long ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Sweeps a z-column of the grid, computing the 7-point __restrict__ f,\n                                               const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const long long z0 = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const long long     const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n        const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n        const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n        const double lap = scaleTerm(xp + xm - (active) op(idx, c0, lap);\n\n        if (z + 1 < z1) {  // roll the z-window forward\n            cm = c0;\n            c0 = cp;\n            cp = (z + 2 < nz) ? f[base + (z + 2) * slice] : c0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                            \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block covers BX cells in x (one full warp per row) and BY rows in y.\n// Each thread sweeps a column of ZT cells in z, keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Grid spacing enters the Laplacian as a division by d*d. Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr long long idx3(const long long x, const long long y, const long long z,\n                                                    const long long nx, const long long ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Sweeps a z-column of the grid, computing the 7-point Laplacian of f with clamped\n// boundary conditions and handing (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op.\ntemplate <typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const long long z0 = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const long long z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const long long x = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const long long y = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const long long xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const long long yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const long long slice = nx * ny;\n    const long long base = yc * nx + xc;\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n\n    double cm, c0, cp;\n    c0 = f[base + z0 * slice];\n    cm = (z0 > 0) ? f[base + (z0 - 1) * slice] : c0;\n    cp = (z0 + 1 < nz) ? f[base + (z0 + 1) * slice] : c0;\n\n    for (long long z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const long long idx = base + z * slice;\n\n        // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n        double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n        if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n        else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n        const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n        const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n        const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n        const double lap = scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n                           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n                           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n\n        if (active) op(idx, c0, lap);\n\n        if (z + 1 < z1) {  // roll the z-window forward\n            cm = c0;\n            c0 = cp;\n            cp = (z + 2 < nz) ? f[base + (z + 2) * slice] : c0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const long long idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double D, const double dt) {\n    laplacianSweep(mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const long long idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t linear_id = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x;\n    if (linear_id >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// A division by k is bit-identical to a multiplication by 1/k exactly when k is a\n// power of two (the reciprocal is then exact).\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double k) {\n    int exp = 0;\n    return k > 0.0 && std::isfinite(k) && std::frexp(k, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    Spacing sp;\n    sp.reciprocal = isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz);\n    sp.kx = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    sp.ky = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    sp.kz = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n    printf(\"Device: %s (%d SMs)\\n\", prop.name, prop.multiProcessorCount);\n\n    // Allocate arrays (device resident; only the final field is copied back)\n    double *d_cold = nullptr, *d_cnew = nullptr, *d_mu = nullptr;\n iterations; ++t) {\n        // Compute chemical potential\n        computeChemicalPotentialKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_cold, d_mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp,\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CekagmKWDJVokvpALJQMr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011fp6xaQBNCudvpdkfh4j3c","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_cuda_r3/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","old_string":"// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps a z-column of the grid, computing the 7-point Laplacian of f with clamped\n// boundary conditions and handing (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op.\ntemplate <typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const long long z0 = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const long long z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const long long x = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const long long y = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const long long xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const long long yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const long long slice = nx * ny;\n    const long long base = yc * nx + xc;\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n\n    double cm, c0, cp;\n    c0 = f[base + z0 * slice];\n    cm = (z0 > 0) ? f[base + (z0 - 1) * slice] : c0;\n    cp = (z0 + 1 < nz) ? f[base + (z0 + 1) * slice] : c0;\n\n    for (long long z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const long long idx = base + z * slice;\n\n        // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n        double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n        if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n        if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n        else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n        const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n        const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n        const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n        const double lap = scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n                           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n                           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n\n        if (active) op(idx, c0, lap);\n\n        if (z + 1 < z1) {  // roll the z-window forward\n            cm = c0;\n            c0 = cp;\n            cp = (z + 2 < nz) ? f[base + (z + 2) * slice] : c0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const long long idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const long long nx, const long long ny, const long long nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double D, const double dt) {\n    laplacianSweep(mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const long long idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}","new_string":"// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\ntemplate <typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const I levels = min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\ntemplate <typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const I levels = min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z);\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n            for (int k = 0; k < levels; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\ntemplate <typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const int levels = static_cast<int>(min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z));\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n                    const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                    const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                    if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double D, const double dt) {\n    laplacianSweep(mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t linear_id = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x;\n    if (linear_id >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block covers BX cells in x (one full warp per row) and BY rows in y.\n// Each thread sweeps a column of ZT cells in z, keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Grid spacing enters the Laplacian as a division by d*d. Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\ntemplate <typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const int levels = static_cast<int>(min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z));\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n                    const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                    const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                    if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const critical path).\ntemplate <typename I>\nstatic void runTimeSteps(double*& d_cold, double*& d_cnew, double* const d_mu,\n                         const dim3 grid, const dim3 block,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int zTile,\n                         const Spacing sp, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n iterations; ++t) {\n        // Compute chemical potential\n        computeChemicalPotentialKernel<I><<<grid, block>>>(d_cold, d_mu, inx, iny, inz, zTile, sp,\n // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool      nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    Spacing sp;\n    sp.reciprocal = isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz);\n    sp.kx = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    sp.ky = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    sp.kz = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    int device = 0;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                            \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block covers BX cells in x (one full warp per row) and BY rows in y.\n// Each thread sweeps a column of ZT cells in z, keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Grid spacing enters the Laplacian as a division by d*d. Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\ntemplate <typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n           scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const int levels = static_cast<int>(min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z));\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n                    const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                    const double lap = laplacianAt(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                    if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double D, const double dt) {\n    laplacianSweep(mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t linear_id = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x;\n    if (linear_id >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\n// UNIT_SPACING drops the (then exactly  const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    Spacing sp;\n    sp.reciprocal = isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz);\n    sp.kx = sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * 1) / zTile) < targetBlocks) zTile /= 2;\n\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + BX - 1) / BX), static_cast<unsigned>((ny + BY - 1) / BY),\n \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block covers BX cells in x (one full warp per row) and BY rows in y.\n// Each thread sweeps a column of ZT cells in z, keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Grid spacing enters the Laplacian as a division by d*d. Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\n// UNIT_SPACING drops the (then exactly neutral) division by d*d == 1.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    if constexpr (UNIT_SPACING) {\n        return (xp + xm - twoc) + (yp + ym - twoc) + (cp + cm - twoc);\n    } else {\n        return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n    }\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const int levels = static_cast<int>(min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z));\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt<UNIT_SPACING>(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\n// UNIT_SPACING drops the (then exactly neutral) division by d*d == 1.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    if constexpr (UNIT_SPACING) {\n        return (xp + xm - twoc) + (yp + ym - twoc) + (cp + cm - twoc);\n    } else {\n        return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n    }\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const int levels = static_cast<int>(min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z));\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt<UNIT_SPACING>(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n                    const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                    const double lap = laplacianAt<UNIT_SPACING>(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                    if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep<UNIT_SPACING>(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double D, const double dt) {\n    laplacianSweep<UNIT_SPACING>(mu, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, double, const double lap) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s to a multiplication by 1/k exactly when k is a\n// power of two (the reciprocal is then exact).\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double k) {\n    int exp = 0;\n    return k > 0.0 && std::isfinite(k) && std::frexp(k, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// All parameters the time loop needs to hand to the kernels.\nstruct SimParams {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz;\n    int zTile;\n    Spacing sp;\n    bool unitSpacing;\n Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\n// UNIT_SPACING drops the (then exactly neutral) division by d*d == 1.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    if constexpr (UNIT_SPACING) {\n        return (xp + xm - twoc) + (yp + ym - twoc) + (cp + cm - twoc);\n    } else {\n        return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n    }\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt<UNIT_SPACING>(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    laplacianSweep<UNIT_SPACING>(c, nx, ny, nz, zTile, sp, [&](const I idx, const double cv, const double lap) {\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny,  cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t linear_id = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x;\n    if (linear_id >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                               \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block covers BX cells in x (one full warp per row) and BY rows in y.\n// Each thread sweeps a column of ZT cells in z, keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n// Grid spacing enters the Laplacian as a division by d*d. Dividing is exactly\n// equivalent to multiplying by the reciprocal iff d*d is a power of two, which is\n// checked on the host; otherwise the (slower) division is used to keep results identical.\nstruct Spacing {\n    double kx, ky, kz;  // either d*d or its reciprocal\n    int reciprocal;\n};\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleTerm(const double v, const double k, const int reciprocal) {\n    return reciprocal ? v * k : v / k;\n}\n\n// Number of z-levels a thread evaluates per loop iteration. The z-column values are\n// held in registers, so the independent levels give the (long-latency) FP64 pipeline\n// several instruction streams to interleave.\nstatic constexpr int ZU = 4;\n\n// 7-point Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, for the cell at (xc, yc) whose\n// z-column values f(z-1), f(z), f(z+1) are passed in as cm, c0, cp.\n// UNIT_SPACING drops the (then exactly neutral) division by d*d == 1.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const I idx,\n                                              const I nx, const I ny, const I xc, const I yc,\n                                              const double cm, const double c0, const double cp,\n                                              const Spacing sp) {\n    // x-neighbours come from the neighbouring lanes; only the warp edges load.\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    double xm = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    double xp = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, c0, 1);\n    if (lane == 0) xm = (xc > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : c0;\n    if (xc == nx - 1) xp = c0;\n    else if (lane == BX - 1) xp = f[idx + 1];\n\n    const double ym = (yc > 0) ? f[idx - nx] : c0;\n    const double yp = (yc < ny - 1) ? f[idx + nx] : c0;\n\n    const double twoc = 2.0 * c0;\n    if constexpr (UNIT_SPACING) {\n        return (xp + xm - twoc) + (yp + ym - twoc) + (cp + cm - twoc);\n    } else {\n        return scaleTerm(xp + xm - twoc, sp.kx, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(yp + ym - twoc, sp.ky, sp.reciprocal) +\n               scaleTerm(cp + cm - twoc, sp.kz, sp.reciprocal);\n    }\n}\n\n// The field is laid out as idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x.\n//\n// Sweeps the z-range assigned to this block, computing the Laplacian of f and handing\n// (index, centre value, Laplacian) to op for every cell.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I, typename Op>\n__device__ __forceinline__ void laplacianSweep(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                               const I nx, const I ny, const I nz,\n                                               const int zTile, const Spacing sp, Op op) {\n    const I z0 = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.z) * zTile;\n    if (z0 >= nz) return;  // uniform for the whole block: warp-wide shuffles stay safe\n    const I z1 = min(z0 + zTile, nz);\n\n    const I x = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.x) * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const I y = static_cast<I>(blockIdx.y) * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n    // Out-of-range threads keep running (they take part in the shuffles) but read\n    // clamped locations and never store.\n    const I xc = active ? x : nx - 1;\n    const I yc = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const I slice = nx * ny;\n    const I base = yc * nx + xc;\n\n    for (I z = z0; z < z1; z += ZU) {\n        const int levels = static_cast<int>(min(static_cast<I>(ZU), z1 - z));\n\n        // z-column window: w[k] holds f(z - 1 + k), clamped at the domain boundary.\n        double w[ZU + 2];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < ZU + 2; ++k) {\n            const I zz = min(max(z - 1 + k, static_cast<I>(0)), nz - 1);\n            w[k] = f[base + zz * slice];\n        }\n\n        if (levels == ZU) {  // common case: fully unrolled, ZU independent streams\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                const I idx = base + (z + k) * slice;\n                const double lap = laplacianAt<UNIT_SPACING>(f, idx, nx, ny, xc, yc, w[k], w[k + 1], w[k + 2], sp);\n                if (active) op(idx, w[k + 1], lap);\n            }\n        } else {  // partial tail tile\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int k = 0; k < ZU; ++k) {\n                if (k < levels) {\n computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, const I nz, const int zTile, const Spacing sp,\n    const double gamma, const 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -\n                  gamma * lap;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu,\n    const I nx, const I ny, linear_id = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x;\n    if (linear_id >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n    c[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const std::frexp(k, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// All parameters the time loop needs to hand to the kernels.\nstruct SimParams {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz;\n    int zTile;\n    Spacing sp;\n    bool unitSpacing;\n critical path).\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING, typename I>\nstatic void runTimeSteps(double*& d_cold, double*& d_cnew, double* const d_mu,\n                         const dim3 grid, const dim3 block, const SimParams& p) {\n    const I nx = static_cast<I>(p.nx), ny = static_cast<I>(p.ny), nz = static_cast<I>(p.nz);\n    for (int t = 0; t < p.iterations; ++t) {\n        // Compute chemical {\n    // 32-bit indices suffice as long as every address (including the +-nx halo offsets)\n    // stays inside the signed 32-bit range.\n    const bool narrow = (p.nx * p.ny * p.nz + 2 * p.nx * = params.sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    params.sp.ky = params.sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    params.sp.kz = params.sp.reciprocal ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n
